<h3>perlretut - Perl regular expressions tutorial</h3>
<dl>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Part 1: The basics</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Simple word matching</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Using character classes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Matching this or that</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Grouping things and hierarchical matching</dt>
<dd> <p> 0. Start with the first letter in the string <code>&#8216;a&#8217;</code>, 1. Try the first alternative in the first group <code>&#8216;abd&#8217;</code>, 2.<code>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;</code>Match <code>&#8216;a&#8217;</code> followed by<br/>
 <code>&#8216;b&#8217;</code>. So far so good, 3. <code>&#8216;d&#8217;</code> in the regexp doesn&#8217;t match <code>&#8216;c&#8217;</code> in the string - a dead end. So backtrack two characters and pick the second alternative in the first group <code>&#8216;abc&#8217;</code>, 4.<code>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;</code>Match <code>&#8216;a&#8217;</code> followed by<br/>
 <code>&#8216;b&#8217;</code> followed by <code>&#8216;c&#8217;</code>. We are on a roll and have satisfied the first group. Set <code>$1</code> to <code>&#8216;abc&#8217;</code>, 5 Move on to the second group and pick the first alternative <code>&#8216;df&#8217;</code>, 6 Match the <code>&#8216;d&#8217;</code>, 7. <code>&#8216;f&#8217;</code> in the regexp doesn&#8217;t match <code>&#8216;e&#8217;</code> in the string, so a dead end. Backtrack one character and pick the second alternative in the second group <code>&#8216;d&#8217;</code>, 8. <br/>
&nbsp;<code>&#8216;d&#8217;</code>&nbsp;matches. The second grouping is satisfied, so set&nbsp;<code>$2</code>&nbsp;to&nbsp;<code>&#8216;d&#8217;</code>, 9. We are at the end of the regexp, so we are done! We have matched <code>&#8216;abcd&#8217;</code> out of the string <code>&quot;abcde&quot;</code> </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Extracting matches</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Backreferences</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Relative backreferences</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Named backreferences</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Alternative capture group numbering</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Position information</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Non-capturing groupings</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Matching repetitions</dt>
<dd> <p> 0. Start with the first letter in the string <code>&#8216;t&#8217;</code>, 1.<code>&nbsp;</code>The first<br/>
 quantifier <code>&#8216;.*&#8216;</code> starts out by matching the whole string &quot;<code>&quot;the cat in the hat&quot;</code>&quot;, 2. <code>&#8216;a&#8217;</code> in the regexp element <code>&#8216;at&#8217;</code> doesn&#8217;t match the end of the string. Backtrack one character, 3. <code>&#8216;a&#8217;</code> in the regexp element <code>&#8216;at&#8217;</code> still doesn&#8217;t match the last letter of the string <code>&#8216;t&#8217;</code>, so backtrack one more character, 4.<code>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;</code>Now we can match the <code>&#8216;a&#8217;</code> and the<br/>
 <code>&#8216;t&#8217;</code>, 5. Move on to the third element <code>&#8216;.*&#8216;</code>. Since we are at the end of the string and <code>&#8216;.*&#8216;</code> can match 0 times, assign it the empty string, 6. We are done! </p>
 </dd>

<h3>perlfaq6 - Regular Expressions</h3>
<dl>
<dt>VERSION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>How can I hope to use regular expressions without creating illegible and unmaintainable code?</dt>
<dd> <p> Comments Outside the Regex, Comments Inside the Regex, Different Delimiters </p>
 </dd>
<dt>I&#8217;m having trouble matching over more than one line. What&#8217;s wrong?</dt>
<dd>  </dd>

 </dd>

<h3>perlunicode - Unicode support in Perl</h3>
<dl>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Important Caveats</dt>
<dd> <p> Safest if you <code>&quot;use feature &#8216;unicode_strings&#8217;&quot;</code>, Input and Output Layers, You must convert your non-ASCII, non-UTF-8 Perl scripts to be UTF-8, <code>&quot;use utf8&quot;</code> still needed to enable UTF-8 in scripts, UTF-16 scripts autodetected </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Byte and Character Semantics</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>ASCII Rules versus Unicode Rules</dt>
<dd> <p> When the string has been upgraded to UTF-8, There are additional methods for regular expression patterns </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Extended Grapheme Clusters (Logical characters)</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Unicode Character Properties</dt>
<dd> <p> &quot;\p{All}&quot;, &quot;\p{Alnum}&quot;, &quot;\p{Any}&quot;, &quot;\p{ASCII}&quot;, &quot;\p{Assigned}&quot;, &quot;\p{Blank}&quot;, &quot;\p{Decomposition_Type: Non_Canonical}&quot; (Short: <code>&quot;\p{Dt=NonCanon}&quot;</code>), &quot;\p{Graph}&quot;, &quot;\p{HorizSpace}&quot;, &quot;\p{In=*}&quot;, &quot;\p{PerlSpace}&quot;, &quot;\p{PerlWord}&quot;, &quot;\p{Posix...}&quot;, &quot;\p{Present_In: *}&quot; (Short: <code>&quot;\p{In=*}&quot;</code>), &quot;\p{Print}&quot;, &quot;\p{SpacePerl}&quot;, &quot;\p{Title}&quot; and &quot;\p{Titlecase}&quot;, &quot;\p{Unicode}&quot;, &quot;\p{VertSpace}&quot;, &quot;\p{Word}&quot;, &quot;\p{XPosix...}&quot; </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Wildcards in Property Values</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>User-Defined Character Properties</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>User-Defined Case Mappings (for serious hackers only)</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Character Encodings for Input and Output</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Unicode Regular Expression Support Level</dt>
<dd> <p> [1] <code>&quot;\N{U+...}&quot;</code> and <code>&quot;\x{...}&quot;</code>, [2] <code>&quot;\p{...}&quot;</code> <code>&quot;\P{...}&quot;</code>. This requirement is for a minimal list of properties. Perl supports these and all other Unicode character properties, as R2.7 asks (see ``Unicode Character Properties&#8217;&#8216; above), [3] Perl has <code>&quot;\d&quot;</code> <code>&quot;\D&quot;</code> <code>&quot;\s&quot;</code> <code>&quot;\S&quot;</code> <code>&quot;\w&quot;</code> <code>&quot;\W&quot;</code> <code>&quot;\X&quot;</code> <code>&quot;[:</code>prop <code>:]&quot;</code> <code>&quot;[:^</code>prop <code>:]&quot;</code>, plus all the properties specified by &lt;<a rel="nofollow" href="http://www.unicode.org/reports/tr18/#Compatibility_Properties">http://www.unicode.org/reports/tr18/#Compatibility_Properties</a>&gt;. These are described above in ``Other Properties&#8217;&#8216;, [4], Regular expression lookahead, [5] <code>&quot;\b&quot;</code> <code>&quot;\B&quot;</code> meet most, but not all, the details of this requirement, but <code>&quot;\b{wb}&quot;</code> and <code>&quot;\B{wb}&quot;</code> do, as well as the stricter R2.3, [6], [7], [8] UTF-8/UTF-EBDDIC used in Perl allows not only <code>&quot;U+10000&quot;</code> to <code>&quot;U+10FFFF&quot;</code> but also beyond <code>&quot;U+10FFFF&quot;</code>, [9] Unicode has rewritten this portion of UTS#18 to say that getting canonical equivalence (see UAX#15 ``Unicode Normalization Forms&#8217;&#8216; &lt;<a rel="nofollow" href="http://www.unicode.org/reports/tr15">http://www.unicode.org/reports/tr15</a>&gt;) is basically to be done at the programmer level. Use NFD to write both your regular expressions and text to match them against (you can use Unicode::Normalize), [10] Perl has <code>&quot;\X&quot;</code> and <code>&quot;\b{gcb}&quot;</code> but we don&#8217;t have a ``Grapheme Cluster Mode&#8217;&#8216;, [11] see UAX#29 ``Unicode Text Segmentation&#8217;&#8216; &lt;<a rel="nofollow" href="http://www.unicode.org/reports/tr29">http://www.unicode.org/reports/tr29</a>&gt;,, [12] see ``Wildcards in Property Values&#8217;&#8216; above, [13] Perl has Unicode::Collate, but it isn&#8217;t integrated with regular expressions. See UTS#10 ``Unicode Collation Algorithms&#8217;&#8216; &lt;<a rel="nofollow" href="http://www.unicode.org/reports/tr10">http://www.unicode.org/reports/tr10</a>&gt;, [14] Perl has <code>&quot;(?&lt;=x)&quot;</code> and <code>&quot;(?=x)&quot;</code>, but this requirement says that it should be possible to specify that matches may occur only in a substring with the lookaheads and lookbehinds able to see beyond that matchable portion, [15] Perl has user-defined properties (``User-Defined Character Properties&#8217;&#8216;) to look at single code points in ways beyond Unicode, and it might be possible, though probably not very clean, to use code blocks and things like <code>&quot;(?(DEFINE)...)&quot;</code> (see perlre) to do more specialized matching </p>
 </dd>

<dt>When Unicode Does Not Happen</dt>

<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Forcing Unicode in Perl (Or Unforcing Unicode in Perl)</dt>

<h3>perluniprops - Index of Unicode Version 12.1.0 character properties in Perl</h3>
<dl>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Properties accessible through &quot;\p{}&quot; and &quot;\P{}&quot;</dt>
<dd> <p> Single form (<code>&quot;\p{name}&quot;</code>) tighter rules:, white space adjacent to a non-word character, underscores separating digits in numbers, Compound form (<code>&quot;\p{name=value}&quot;</code> or <code>&quot;\p{name:value}&quot;</code>) tighter rules:, Stabilized, Deprecated, Obsolete, Discouraged, <code>*</code> is a wild-card, <code>(\d+)</code> in the info column gives the number of Unicode code points matched by this property, <code>D</code> means this is deprecated, <code>O</code> means this is obsolete, <code>S</code> means this is stabilized, <code>T</code> means tighter (stricter) name matching applies, <code>X</code> means use of this form is discouraged, and may not be stable </p>
 </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Legal &quot;\p{}&quot; and &quot;\P{}&quot; constructs that match no characters</dt>
<dd> <p> \p{Canonical_Combining_Class=Attached_Below_Left}, \p{Canonical_Combining_Class=CCC133}, \p{Grapheme_Cluster_Break=E_Base}, \p{Grapheme_Cluster_Break=E_Base_GAZ}, \p{Grapheme_Cluster_Break=E_Modifier}, \p{Grapheme_Cluster_Break=Glue_After_Zwj}, \p{Word_Break=E_Base}, \p{Word_Break=E_Base_GAZ}, \p{Word_Break=E_Modifier}, \p{Word_Break=Glue_After_Zwj} </p>
 </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Properties accessible through Unicode::UCD</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Properties accessible through other means</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Unicode character properties that are NOT accepted by Perl</dt>
<dd> <p> <i>Expands_On_NFC</i> (XO_NFC), <i>Expands_On_NFD</i> (XO_NFD), <i>Expands_On_NFKC</i> (XO_NFKC), <i>Expands_On_NFKD</i> (XO_NFKD), <i>Extended_Pictographic</i> (XPG), <i>Grapheme_Link</i> (Gr_Link), <i>Jamo_Short_Name</i> (JSN), <i>Other_Alphabetic</i> (OAlpha), <i>Other_Default_Ignorable_Code_Point</i> (ODI), <i>Other_Grapheme_Extend</i> (OGr_Ext), <i>Other_ID_Continue</i> (OIDC), <i>Other_ID_Start</i> (OIDS), <i>Other_Lowercase</i> (OLower), <i>Other_Math</i> (OMath), <i>Other_Uppercase</i> (OUpper), <i>Script=Katakana_Or_Hiragana</i> (sc=Hrkt), <i>Script_Extensions=Katakana_Or_Hiragana</i> (scx=Hrkt) </p>
 </dd>

<dt>What should I make into a module?</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Step-by-step: Preparing the ground</dt>
<dd> <p> Look around, Check it&#8217;s new, Discuss the need, Choose a name, Check again </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Step-by-step: Making the module</dt>
<dd> <p> Start with <i>module-starter</i> or <i>h2xs</i>, Use strict and warnings, Use Carp, Use Exporter - wisely!, Use plain old documentation, Write tests, Write the <i></i><i>README</i><i></i>, Write <i>Changes</i> </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Step-by-step: Distributing your module</dt>
<dd> <p> Get a CPAN user ID, <code>&quot;perl Makefile.PL; make test; make distcheck; make dist&quot;</code>, Upload the tarball, Fix bugs! </p>
 </dd></dl>

USE_PERLIO, <code>PerlIO_stdin()</code>, <code>PerlIO_stdout()</code>, <code>PerlIO_stderr()</code>, <code>PerlIO_open(path, mode)</code>, <code>PerlIO_fdopen(fd,mode)</code>, <code>PerlIO_reopen(path,mode,f)</code>, <code>PerlIO_printf(f,fmt,...)</code>, <code>PerlIO_vprintf(f,fmt,a)</code>, <code>PerlIO_stdoutf(fmt,...)</code>, <code>PerlIO_read(f,buf,count)</code>, <code>PerlIO_write(f,buf,count)</code>, <code>PerlIO_close(f)</code>, <code>PerlIO_puts(f,s)</code>, <code>PerlIO_putc(f,c)</code>, <code>PerlIO_ungetc(f,c)</code>, <code>PerlIO_getc(f)</code>, <code>PerlIO_eof(f)</code>, <code>PerlIO_error(f)</code>, <code>PerlIO_fileno(f)</code>, <code>PerlIO_clearerr(f)</code>, <code>PerlIO_flush(f)</code>, <code>PerlIO_seek(f,offset,whence)</code>, <code>PerlIO_tell(f)</code>, <code>PerlIO_getpos(f,p)</code>, <code>PerlIO_setpos(f,p)</code>, <code>PerlIO_rewind(f)</code>, <code>PerlIO_tmpfile()</code>, <code>PerlIO_setlinebuf(f)</code> </p>

<h3>perldelta - what is new for perl v5.30.0</h3>
<dl>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Notice</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Core Enhancements</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Limited variable length lookbehind in regular expression pattern matching is now experimentally supported</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>The upper limit &quot;n&quot; specifiable in a regular expression quantifier of the form &quot;{m,n}&quot; has been doubled to 65534</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Unicode 12.1 is supported</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Wildcards in Unicode property value specifications are now partially supported</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>qr&#8217;\N{name}&#8216; is now supported</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Turkic UTF-8 locales are now seamlessly supported</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>It is now possible to compile perl to always use thread-safe locale operations.</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Eliminate opASSIGN macro usage from core</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;-Drv&quot; now means something on &quot;-DDEBUGGING&quot; builds</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Incompatible Changes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Assigning non-zero to $[ is fatal</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Delimiters must now be graphemes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Some formerly deprecated uses of an unescaped left brace &quot;{&quot; in regular expression patterns are now illegal</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Previously deprecated <code>sysread()</code>/<code>syswrite()</code> on :utf8 handles is now fatal</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>my()</code> in false conditional prohibited</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Fatalize $* and $#</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Fatalize unqualified use of <code>dump()</code></dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Remove <code>File::Glob::glob()</code></dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;pack()&quot; no longer can return malformed UTF-8</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Any set of digits in the Common script are legal in a script run of another script</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>JSON::PP enables allow_nonref by default</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Deprecations</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>In XS code, use of various macros dealing with UTF-8.</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Performance Enhancements</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Modules and Pragmata</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>

<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd></dl>

<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd></dl>

<h3>perl5280delta - what is new for perl v5.28.0</h3>
<dl>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Core Enhancements</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Unicode 10.0 is supported</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;delete&quot; on key/value hash slices</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Experimentally, there are now alphabetic synonyms for some regular expression assertions</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Mixed Unicode scripts are now detectable</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>In-place editing with &quot;perl -i&quot; is now safer</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Initialisation of aggregate state variables</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Full-size inode numbers</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>The &quot;sprintf&quot; %j format size modifier is now available with pre-C99 compilers</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Close-on-exec flag set atomically</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>String- and number-specific bitwise ops are no longer experimental</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Locales are now thread-safe on systems that support them</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>New read-only predefined variable &quot;${^SAFE_LOCALES}&quot;</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Security</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>[CVE-2017-12837] Heap buffer overflow in regular expression compiler</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>[CVE-2017-12883] Buffer over-read in regular expression parser</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>[CVE-2017-12814] $ENV{$key} stack buffer overflow on Windows</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Default Hash Function Change</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Incompatible Changes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Subroutine attribute and signature order</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Comma-less variable lists in formats are no longer allowed</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>The &quot;:locked&quot; and &quot;:unique&quot; attributes have been removed</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;\N{}&quot; with nothing between the braces is now illegal</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Opening the same symbol as both a file and directory handle is no longer allowed</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Use of bare &quot;&lt;&lt;&quot; to mean &quot;&lt;&lt;&quot;&quot;&quot; is no longer allowed</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Setting $/ to a reference to a non-positive integer no longer allowed</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Unicode code points with values exceeding &quot;IV_MAX&quot; are now fatal</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>The &quot;B::OP::terse&quot; method has been removed</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Use of inherited AUTOLOAD for non-methods is no longer allowed</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Use of strings with code points over 0xFF is not allowed for bitwise string operators</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Setting &quot;${^ENCODING}&quot; to a defined value is now illegal</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Backslash no longer escapes colon in PATH for the &quot;-S&quot; switch</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>the -DH (DEBUG_H) misfeature has been removed</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Yada-yada is now strictly a statement</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Sort algorithm can no longer be specified</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Over-radix digits in floating point literals</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Return type of &quot;unpackstring()&quot;</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Deprecations</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Use of &quot;vec&quot; on strings with code points above 0xFF is deprecated</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Some uses of unescaped &quot;{&quot; in regexes are no longer fatal</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Use of unescaped &quot;{&quot; immediately after a &quot;(&quot; in regular expression patterns is deprecated</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Assignment to $[ will be fatal in Perl 5.30</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>hostname()</code> won&#8217;t accept arguments in Perl 5.32</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Module removals</dt>
<dd> <p> B::Debug, Locale::Codes and its associated Country, Currency and Language modules </p>
 </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Performance Enhancements</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Modules and Pragmata</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Removal of use vars</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Use of DynaLoader changed to XSLoader in many modules</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Updated Modules and Pragmata</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Removed Modules and Pragmata</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Documentation</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Changes to Existing Documentation</dt>
<dd> <p> ``Variable length lookbehind not implemented in regex m/%s/&#8216;&#8216; in perldiag, ``Use of state <code>$_</code> is experimental&#8217;&#8216; in perldiag </p>
 </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Diagnostics</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>New Diagnostics</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Changes to Existing Diagnostics</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Utility Changes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>perlbug</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Configuration and Compilation</dt>
<dd> <p> C89 requirement, New probes, HAS_BUILTIN_ADD_OVERFLOW, HAS_BUILTIN_MUL_OVERFLOW, HAS_BUILTIN_SUB_OVERFLOW, HAS_THREAD_SAFE_NL_LANGINFO_L, HAS_LOCALECONV_L, HAS_MBRLEN, HAS_MBRTOWC, HAS_MEMRCHR, HAS_NANOSLEEP, HAS_STRNLEN, HAS_STRTOLD_L, I_WCHAR </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Testing</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Packaging</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Platform Support</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>

<h3>perl5260delta - what is new for perl v5.26.0</h3>
<dl>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Notice</dt>
<dd> <p> <code>&quot;.&quot;</code> no longer in <code>@INC</code>, <code>&quot;do&quot;</code> may now warn, In regular expression patterns, a literal left brace <code>&quot;{&quot;</code> should be escaped </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Core Enhancements</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Lexical subroutines are no longer experimental</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Indented Here-documents</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>New regular expression modifier &quot;/xx&quot;</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;@{^CAPTURE}&quot;, &quot;%{^CAPTURE}&quot;, and &quot;%{^CAPTURE_ALL}&quot;</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Declaring a reference to a variable</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Unicode 9.0 is now supported</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Use of &quot;\p{<i>script</i>}&quot; uses the improved Script_Extensions property</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Perl can now do default collation in UTF-8 locales on platforms that support it</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Better locale collation of strings containing embedded &quot;NUL&quot; characters</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;CORE&quot; subroutines for hash and array functions callable via reference</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>New Hash Function For 64-bit Builds</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Security</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Removal of the current directory (&quot;.&quot;) from @INC</dt>
<dd> <p> <i>Configure -Udefault_inc_excludes_dot</i>, <code>&quot;PERL_USE_UNSAFE_INC&quot;</code>, A new deprecation warning issued by <code>&quot;do&quot;</code>, Script authors, Installing and using CPAN modules, Module Authors </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Escaped colons and relative paths in PATH</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>New &quot;-Di&quot; switch is now required for PerlIO debugging output</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Incompatible Changes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Unescaped literal &quot;{&quot; characters in regular expression patterns are no longer permissible</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;scalar(%hash)&quot; return signature changed</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;keys&quot; returned from an lvalue subroutine</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>The &quot;${^ENCODING}&quot; facility has been removed</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;POSIX::tmpnam()&quot; has been removed</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>require ::Foo::Bar is now illegal.</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Literal control character variable names are no longer permissible</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;NBSP&quot; is no longer permissible in &quot;\N{...}&quot;</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Deprecations</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>String delimiters that aren&#8217;t stand-alone graphemes are now deprecated</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;\c <i>X</i>&quot; that maps to a printable is no longer deprecated</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Performance Enhancements</dt>
<dd> <p> New Faster Hash Function on 64 bit builds, readline is faster </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Modules and Pragmata</dt>
<dd>  </dd>

<h3>perl5240delta - what is new for perl v5.24.0</h3>
<dl>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Core Enhancements</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Postfix dereferencing is no longer experimental</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Unicode 8.0 is now supported</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>perl will now croak when closing an in-place output file fails</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>New &quot;\b{lb}&quot; boundary in regular expressions</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;qr/(?[ ])/&quot; now works in UTF-8 locales</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Integer shift (&quot;&lt;&lt;&quot; and &quot;&gt;&gt;&quot;) now more explicitly defined</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>printf and sprintf now allow reordered precision arguments</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>More fields provided to &quot;sigaction&quot; callback with &quot;SA_SIGINFO&quot;</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Hashbang redirection to Perl 6</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Security</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Set proper umask before calling <a href="/docs/linux/man/3-mkstemp/">mkstemp</a>(3)</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Fix out of boundary access in Win32 path handling</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Fix loss of taint in canonpath</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Avoid accessing uninitialized memory in win32 &quot;crypt()&quot;</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Remove duplicate environment variables from &quot;environ&quot;</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Incompatible Changes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>The &quot;autoderef&quot; feature has been removed</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Lexical $_ has been removed</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;qr/\b{wb}/&quot; is now tailored to Perl expectations</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Regular expression compilation errors</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;qr/\N{}/&quot; now disallowed under &quot;use re &quot;strict&quot;&quot;</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Nested declarations are now disallowed</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>The &quot;/\C/&quot; character class has been removed.</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;chdir(&#8216;&#8216;)&quot; no longer chdirs home</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>ASCII characters in variable names must now be all visible</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>An off by one issue in $Carp::MaxArgNums has been fixed</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Only blanks and tabs are now allowed within &quot;[...]&quot; within &quot;(?[...])&quot;.</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Deprecations</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Using code points above the platform&#8217;s &quot;IV_MAX&quot; is now deprecated</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Doing bitwise operations on strings containing code points above 0xFF is deprecated</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;sysread()&quot;, &quot;syswrite()&quot;, &quot;recv()&quot; and &quot;send()&quot; are deprecated on :utf8 handles</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Performance Enhancements</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Modules and Pragmata</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>

<dd>  </dd></dl>

<dd> <p> AmigaOS, Cygwin, EBCDIC, UTF-EBCDIC extended, EBCDIC <code>&quot;cmp()&quot;</code> and <code>&quot;sort()&quot;</code> fixed for UTF-EBCDIC strings, EBCDIC <code>&quot;tr///&quot;</code> and <code>&quot;y///&quot;</code> fixed for <code>&quot;\N{}&quot;</code>, and <code>&quot;use utf8&quot;</code> ranges, FreeBSD, IRIX, MacOS X, Solaris, Tru64, VMS, Win32, ppc64el, floating point </p>

<h3>perl5220delta - what is new for perl v5.22.0</h3>
<dl>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Core Enhancements</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>New bitwise operators</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>New double-diamond operator</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>New &quot;\b&quot; boundaries in regular expressions</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Non-Capturing Regular Expression Flag</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;use re &#8216;strict&#8217;&quot;</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Unicode 7.0 (with correction) is now supported</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;use locale&quot; can restrict which locale categories are affected</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Perl now supports POSIX 2008 locale currency additions</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Better heuristics on older platforms for determining locale UTF-8ness</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Aliasing via reference</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;prototype&quot; with no arguments</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>New &quot;:const&quot; subroutine attribute</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;fileno&quot; now works on directory handles</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>List form of pipe open implemented for Win32</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Assignment to list repetition</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Infinity and NaN (not-a-number) handling improved</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Floating point parsing has been improved</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Packing infinity or not-a-number into a character is now fatal</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Experimental C Backtrace API</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Security</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Perl is now compiled with &quot;-fstack-protector-strong&quot; if available</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>The Safe module could allow outside packages to be replaced</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Perl is now always compiled with &quot;-D_FORTIFY_SOURCE=2&quot; if available</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Incompatible Changes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Subroutine signatures moved before attributes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;&amp;&quot; and &quot;\&amp;&quot; prototypes accepts only subs</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;use encoding&quot; is now lexical</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>List slices returning empty lists</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;\N{}&quot; with a sequence of multiple spaces is now a fatal error</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;use UNIVERSAL &#8216;...&#8216;&quot; is now a fatal error</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>In double-quotish &quot;\c <i>X</i>&quot;, <i>X</i> must now be a printable ASCII character</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Splitting the tokens &quot;(?&quot; and &quot;(*&quot; in regular expressions is now a fatal compilation error.</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;qr/foo/x&quot; now ignores all Unicode pattern white space</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Comment lines within &quot;(?[ ])&quot; are now ended only by a &quot;\n&quot;</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;(?[...])&quot; operators now follow standard Perl precedence</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Omitting &quot;%&quot; and &quot;@&quot; on hash and array names is no longer permitted</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;$!&quot; text is now in English outside the scope of &quot;use locale&quot;</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;$!&quot; text will be returned in UTF-8 when appropriate</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Support for &quot;?PATTERN?&quot; without explicit operator has been removed</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;defined(@array)&quot; and &quot;defined(%hash)&quot; are now fatal errors</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Using a hash or an array as a reference are now fatal errors</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Changes to the &quot;*&quot; prototype</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Deprecations</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Setting &quot;${^ENCODING}&quot; to anything but &quot;undef&quot;</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Use of non-graphic characters in single-character variable names</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Inlining of &quot;sub () { $var }&quot; with observable side-effects</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Use of multiple &quot;/x&quot; regexp modifiers</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Using a NO-BREAK space in a character alias for &quot;\N{...}&quot; is now deprecated</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>A literal &quot;{&quot; should now be escaped in a pattern</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Making all warnings fatal is discouraged</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Performance Enhancements</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Modules and Pragmata</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>

<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd></dl>

<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd></dl>

<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd></dl>

<dd>  </dd>

<dl>
<dt><i>find2perl</i>, <i>s2p</i> and <i>a2p</i> removal</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>h2ph</dt>
<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd></dl>

<dd>  </dd></dl>

<h3>perl5200delta - what is new for perl v5.20.0</h3>
<dl>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Core Enhancements</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Experimental Subroutine signatures</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;sub&quot;s now take a &quot;prototype&quot; attribute</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>More consistent prototype parsing</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;rand&quot; now uses a consistent random number generator</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>New slice syntax</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Experimental Postfix Dereferencing</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Unicode 6.3 now supported</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>New &quot;\p{Unicode}&quot; regular expression pattern property</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Better 64-bit support</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;use locale&quot; now works on UTF-8 locales</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;use locale&quot; now compiles on systems without locale ability</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>More locale initialization fallback options</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;-DL&quot; runtime option now added for tracing locale setting</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>-F</code> now implies <code>-a</code> and <code>-a</code> implies <code>-n</code></dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>$a and $b warnings exemption</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Security</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Avoid possible read of <code>free()</code>d memory during parsing</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Incompatible Changes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>&quot;do&quot; can no longer be used to call subroutines</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Quote-like escape changes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Tainting happens under more circumstances; now conforms to documentation</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;\p{}&quot;, &quot;\P{}&quot; matching has changed for non-Unicode code points.</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;\p{All}&quot; has been expanded to match all possible code points</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Data::Dumper&#8217;s output may change</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Locale decimal point character no longer leaks outside of &quot;use locale&quot; scope</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Assignments of Windows sockets error codes to $! now prefer <i>errno.h</i> values over <code>WSAGetLastError()</code> values</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Functions &quot;PerlIO_vsprintf&quot; and &quot;PerlIO_sprintf&quot; have been removed</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Deprecations</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>The &quot;/\C/&quot; character class</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Literal control characters in variable names</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>References to non-integers and non-positive integers in $/</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Character matching routines in POSIX</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Interpreter-based threads are now <i>discouraged</i></dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Module removals</dt>
<dd> <p> CGI and its associated CGI:: packages, inc::latest, Package::Constants, Module::Build and its associated Module::Build:: packages </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Utility removals</dt>
<dd> <p> find2perl, s2p, a2p </p>
 </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Performance Enhancements</dt>
<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd>

 </dd>

<dt>Internal Changes</dt>

<dt>Selected Bug Fixes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd>
<dl>

<h3>perl5180delta - what is new for perl v5.18.0</h3>
<dl>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Core Enhancements</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>New mechanism for experimental features</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Hash overhaul</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Upgrade to Unicode 6.2</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Character name aliases may now include non-Latin1-range characters</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>New DTrace probes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;${^LAST_FH}&quot;</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Regular Expression Set Operations</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Lexical subroutines</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Computed Labels</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>More CORE:: subs</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;kill&quot; with negative signal names</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Security</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>See also: hash overhaul</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;Storable&quot; security warning in documentation</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;Locale::Maketext&quot; allowed code injection via a malicious template</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Avoid calling memset with a negative count</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Incompatible Changes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>See also: hash overhaul</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>An unknown character name in &quot;\N{...}&quot; is now a syntax error</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Formerly deprecated characters in &quot;\N{}&quot; character name aliases are now errors.</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;\N{BELL}&quot; now refers to U+1F514 instead of U+0007</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>New Restrictions in Multi-Character Case-Insensitive Matching in Regular Expression Bracketed Character Classes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Explicit rules for variable names and identifiers</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Vertical tabs are now whitespace</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;/(?{})/&quot; and &quot;/(??{})/&quot; have been heavily reworked</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Stricter parsing of substitution replacement</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;given&quot; now aliases the global $_</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>The smartmatch family of features are now experimental</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Lexical $_ is now experimental</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>readline()</code> with &quot;$/ = \N&quot; now reads N characters, not N bytes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Overridden &quot;glob&quot; is now passed one argument</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Here doc parsing</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Alphanumeric operators must now be separated from the closing delimiter of regular expressions</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>qw(...) can no longer be used as parentheses</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Interaction of lexical and default warnings</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;state sub&quot; and &quot;our sub&quot;</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Defined values stored in environment are forced to byte strings</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;require&quot; dies for unreadable files</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;gv_fetchmeth_*&quot; and SUPER</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;split&quot;&#8216;s first argument is more consistently interpreted</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Deprecations</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Module removals</dt>
<dd> <p> encoding, Archive::Extract, B::Lint, B::Lint::Debug, CPANPLUS and all included <code>&quot;CPANPLUS::*&quot;</code> modules, Devel::InnerPackage, Log::Message, Log::Message::Config, Log::Message::Handlers, Log::Message::Item, Log::Message::Simple, Module::Pluggable, Module::Pluggable::Object, Object::Accessor, Pod::LaTeX, Term::UI, Term::UI::History </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Deprecated Utilities</dt>

<dd>  </dd>

<dt>User-defined charnames with surprising whitespace</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Various XS-callable functions are now deprecated</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Certain rare uses of backslashes within regexes are now deprecated</dt>
<dd>  </dd>

<h3>perl561delta - what's new for perl v5.6.1</h3>
<dl>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Summary of changes between 5.6.0 and 5.6.1</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Security Issues</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Core bug fixes</dt>
<dd> <p> <code>&quot;UNIVERSAL::isa()&quot;</code>, Memory leaks, Numeric conversions, qw(a\\b), <code>caller()</code>, Bugs in regular expressions, ``slurp&#8217;&#8216; mode, Autovivification of symbolic references to special variables, Lexical warnings, Spurious warnings and errors, <code>glob()</code>, Tainting, <code>sort()</code>, #line directives, Subroutine prototypes, <code>map()</code>, Debugger, PERL5OPT, <code>chop()</code>, Unicode support, 64-bit support, Compiler, Lvalue subroutines, IO::Socket, File::Find, xsubpp, <code>&quot;no Module;&quot;</code>, Tests </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Core features</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Configuration issues</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Documentation</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Bundled modules</dt>
<dd> <p> B::Concise, File::Temp, Pod::LaTeX, Pod::Text::Overstrike, CGI, CPAN, Class::Struct, DB_File, Devel::Peek, File::Find, Getopt::Long, IO::Poll, IPC::Open3, Math::BigFloat, Math::Complex, Net::Ping, Opcode, Pod::Parser, Pod::Text, SDBM_File, Sys::Syslog, Tie::RefHash, Tie::SubstrHash </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Platform-specific improvements</dt>
<dd> <p> NCR MP-RAS, NonStop-UX </p>
 </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Core Enhancements</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Interpreter cloning, threads, and concurrency</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Lexically scoped warning categories</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Unicode and UTF-8 support</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Support for interpolating named characters</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;our&quot; declarations</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Support for strings represented as a vector of ordinals</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Improved Perl version numbering system</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>New syntax for declaring subroutine attributes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>File and directory handles can be autovivified</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>open()</code> with more than two arguments</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>64-bit support</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Large file support</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Long doubles</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;more bits&quot;</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Enhanced support for <code>sort()</code> subroutines</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;sort $coderef @foo&quot; allowed</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>File globbing implemented internally</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Support for CHECK blocks</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>POSIX character class syntax [: :] supported</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Better pseudo-random number generator</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Improved &quot;qw//&quot; operator</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Better worst-case behavior of hashes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>pack()</code> format &#8216;Z&#8217; supported</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>pack()</code> format modifier &#8216;!&#8216; supported</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>pack()</code> and <code>unpack()</code> support counted strings</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Comments in <code>pack()</code> templates</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Weak references</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Binary numbers supported</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Lvalue subroutines</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Some arrows may be omitted in calls through references</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Boolean assignment operators are legal lvalues</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>exists()</code> is supported on subroutine names</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>exists()</code> and <code>delete()</code> are supported on array elements</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Pseudo-hashes work better</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Automatic flushing of output buffers</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Better diagnostics on meaningless filehandle operations</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Where possible, buffered data discarded from duped input filehandle</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>eof()</code> has the same old magic as &lt;&gt;</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>binmode()</code> can be used to set :crlf and :raw modes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;-T&quot; filetest recognizes UTF-8 encoded files as &quot;text&quot;</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>system()</code>, backticks and pipe open now reflect <code>exec()</code> failure</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Improved diagnostics</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Diagnostics follow STDERR</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>More consistent close-on-exec behavior</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>syswrite()</code> ease-of-use</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Better syntax checks on parenthesized unary operators</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Bit operators support full native integer width</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Improved security features</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>More functional bareword prototype (*)</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;require&quot; and &quot;do&quot; may be overridden</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>$^X variables may now have names longer than one character</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>New variable $^C reflects &quot;-c&quot; switch</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>New variable $^V contains Perl version as a string</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Optional Y2K warnings</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Arrays now always interpolate into double-quoted strings</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>@- and @+ provide starting/ending offsets of regex submatches</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>

<dd>  </dd>

<dd> <p> attributes, B, Benchmark, ByteLoader, constant, charnames, Data::Dumper, DB, DB_File, Devel::DProf, Devel::Peek, Dumpvalue, DynaLoader, English, Env, Fcntl, File::Compare, File::Find, File::Glob, File::Spec, File::Spec::Functions, Getopt::Long, IO, JPL, lib, Math::BigInt, Math::Complex, Math::Trig, Pod::Parser, Pod::InputObjects, Pod::Checker, podchecker, Pod::ParseUtils, Pod::Find, Pod::Select, podselect, Pod::Usage, pod2usage, Pod::Text and Pod::Man, SDBM_File, Sys::Syslog, Sys::Hostname, Term::ANSIColor, Time::Local, Win32, XSLoader, DBM Filters </p>

<dt>New or Changed Diagnostics</dt>
<dd> <p> ``%s&#8217;&#8216; variable <code>%s</code> masks earlier declaration in same <code>%s</code>, ``my sub&#8217;&#8216; not yet implemented, ``our&#8217;&#8216; variable <code>%s</code> redeclared, &#8216;!&#8216; allowed only after types <code>%s</code>, / cannot take a count, / must be followed by a, A or Z, / must be followed by a*, A* or Z*, / must follow a numeric type, /%s/: Unrecognized escape \\%c passed through, /%s/: Unrecognized escape \\%c in character class passed through, /%s/ should probably be written as ``%s&#8217;&#8216;, %s() called too early to check prototype, <code>%s</code> argument is not a HASH or ARRAY element, <code>%s</code> argument is not a HASH or ARRAY element or slice, <code>%s</code> argument is not a subroutine name, <code>%s</code> package attribute may clash with future reserved word: <code>%s</code>, (in cleanup) <code>%s</code>, &lt;&gt; should be quotes, Attempt to join self, Bad evalled substitution pattern, Bad <code>realloc()</code> ignored, Bareword found in conditional, Binary number &gt; 0b11111111111111111111111111111111 non-portable, Bit vector size &gt; 32 non-portable, Buffer overflow in prime_env_iter: <code>%s</code>, Can&#8217;t check filesystem of script ``%s&#8217;&#8216;, Can&#8217;t declare class for non-scalar <code>%s</code> in ``%s&#8217;&#8216;, Can&#8217;t declare <code>%s</code> in ``%s&#8217;&#8216;, Can&#8217;t ignore signal CHLD, forcing to default, Can&#8217;t modify non-lvalue subroutine call, Can&#8217;t read CRTL environ, Can&#8217;t remove <code>%s:</code> <code>%s</code>, skipping file, Can&#8217;t return <code>%s</code> from lvalue subroutine, Can&#8217;t weaken a nonreference, Character class [:%s:] unknown, Character class syntax [%s] belongs inside character classes, Constant is not <code>%s</code> reference, constant(%s): <code>%s</code>, CORE::%s is not a keyword, defined(@array) is deprecated, defined(%hash) is deprecated, Did not produce a valid header, (Did you mean ``local&#8217;&#8216; instead of ``our&#8217;&#8216;?), Document contains no data, entering effective <code>%s</code> failed, false [] range ``%s&#8217;&#8216; in regexp, Filehandle <code>%s</code> opened only for output, <code>flock()</code> on closed filehandle <code>%s</code>, Global symbol ``%s&#8217;&#8216; requires explicit package name, Hexadecimal number &gt; 0xffffffff non-portable, Ill-formed CRTL environ value ``%s&#8217;&#8216;, Ill-formed message in prime_env_iter: |%s|, Illegal binary digit <code>%s</code>, Illegal binary digit <code>%s</code> ignored, Illegal number of bits in vec, Integer overflow in <code>%s</code> number, Invalid <code>%s</code> attribute: <code>%s</code>, Invalid <code>%s</code> attributes: <code>%s</code>, invalid [] range ``%s&#8217;&#8216; in regexp, Invalid separator character <code>%s</code> in attribute list, Invalid separator character <code>%s</code> in subroutine attribute list, leaving effective <code>%s</code> failed, Lvalue subs returning <code>%s</code> not implemented yet, Method <code>%s</code> not permitted, Missing <code>%sbrace</code>%s on \N{}, Missing command in piped open, Missing name in ``my sub&#8217;&#8216;, No <code>%s</code> specified for -%c, No package name allowed for variable <code>%s</code> in ``our&#8217;&#8216;, No space allowed after -%c, no UTC offset information; assuming local time is UTC, Octal number &gt; 037777777777 non-portable, panic: del_backref, panic: kid popen errno read, panic: magic_killbackrefs, Parentheses missing around ``%s&#8217;&#8216; list, Possible unintended interpolation of <code>%s</code> in string, Possible Y2K bug: <code>%s</code>, pragma ``attrs&#8217;&#8216; is deprecated, use ``sub NAME : ATTRS&#8217;&#8216; instead, Premature end of script headers, Repeat count in pack overflows, Repeat count in unpack overflows, <code>realloc()</code> of freed memory ignored, Reference is already weak, setpgrp can&#8217;t take arguments, Strange *+?{} on zero-length expression, switching effective <code>%s</code> is not implemented, This Perl can&#8217;t reset CRTL environ elements (%s), This Perl can&#8217;t set CRTL environ elements (%s=%s), Too late to run <code>%s</code> block, Unknown <code>open()</code> mode &#8216;%s&#8217;, Unknown process <code>%x</code> sent message to prime_env_iter: <code>%s</code>, Unrecognized escape \\%c passed through, Unterminated attribute parameter in attribute list, Unterminated attribute list, Unterminated attribute parameter in subroutine attribute list, Unterminated subroutine attribute list, Value of CLI symbol ``%s&#8217;&#8216; too long, Version number must be a constant number </p>
 </dd>
<dt>New tests</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Incompatible Changes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Perl Source Incompatibilities</dt>
<dd> <p> CHECK is a new keyword, Treatment of list slices of undef has changed, Format of <code>$English::PERL_VERSION</code> is different, Literals of the form <code>1.2.3</code> parse differently, Possibly changed pseudo-random number generator, Hashing function for hash keys has changed, <code>&quot;undef&quot;</code> fails on read only values, Close-on-exec bit may be set on pipe and socket handles, Writing <code>&quot;$$1&quot;</code> to mean <code>&quot;${$}1&quot;</code> is unsupported, <code>delete()</code>, <code>each()</code>, <code>values()</code> and <code>&quot;\(%h)&quot;</code>, vec(EXPR,OFFSET,BITS) enforces powers-of-two BITS, Text of some diagnostic output has changed, <code>&quot;%@&quot;</code> has been removed, Parenthesized <code>not()</code> behaves like a list operator, Semantics of bareword prototype <code>&quot;(*)&quot;</code> have changed, Semantics of bit operators may have changed on 64-bit platforms, More builtins taint their results </p>
 </dd>
<dt>C Source Incompatibilities</dt>
<dd> <p> <code>&quot;PERL_POLLUTE&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;PERL_IMPLICIT_CONTEXT&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;PERL_POLLUTE_MALLOC&quot;</code> </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Compatible C Source API Changes</dt>
<dd> <p> <code>&quot;PATCHLEVEL&quot;</code> is now <code>&quot;PERL_VERSION&quot;</code> </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Binary Incompatibilities</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>

<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd>
<dt>UNICOS/mk CC failures during Configure run</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Arrow operator and arrays</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Experimental features</dt>
<dd> <p> Threads, Unicode, 64-bit support, Lvalue subroutines, Weak references, The pseudo-hash data type, The Compiler suite, Internal implementation of file globbing, The DB module, The regular expression code constructs: </p>
 </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Obsolete Diagnostics</dt>
<dd> <p> Character class syntax [: :] is reserved for future extensions, Ill-formed logical name |%s| in prime_env_iter, In string, @%s now must be written as \@%s, Probable precedence problem on <code>%s</code>, regexp too big, Use of ``$$&lt;digit&gt;&#8216;&#8216; to mean ``${$}&lt;digit&gt;&#8216;&#8216; is deprecated </p>
 </dd>

<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd></dl>
<h3>perl56delta - what's new for perl v5.6.0</h3>
<dl>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Core Enhancements</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Interpreter cloning, threads, and concurrency</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Lexically scoped warning categories</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Unicode and UTF-8 support</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Support for interpolating named characters</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;our&quot; declarations</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Support for strings represented as a vector of ordinals</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Improved Perl version numbering system</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>New syntax for declaring subroutine attributes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>File and directory handles can be autovivified</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>open()</code> with more than two arguments</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>64-bit support</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Large file support</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Long doubles</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;more bits&quot;</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Enhanced support for <code>sort()</code> subroutines</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;sort $coderef @foo&quot; allowed</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>File globbing implemented internally</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Support for CHECK blocks</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>POSIX character class syntax [: :] supported</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Better pseudo-random number generator</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Improved &quot;qw//&quot; operator</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Better worst-case behavior of hashes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>pack()</code> format &#8216;Z&#8217; supported</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>pack()</code> format modifier &#8216;!&#8216; supported</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>pack()</code> and <code>unpack()</code> support counted strings</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Comments in <code>pack()</code> templates</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Weak references</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Binary numbers supported</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Lvalue subroutines</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Some arrows may be omitted in calls through references</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Boolean assignment operators are legal lvalues</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>exists()</code> is supported on subroutine names</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>exists()</code> and <code>delete()</code> are supported on array elements</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Pseudo-hashes work better</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Automatic flushing of output buffers</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Better diagnostics on meaningless filehandle operations</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Where possible, buffered data discarded from duped input filehandle</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>eof()</code> has the same old magic as &lt;&gt;</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>binmode()</code> can be used to set :crlf and :raw modes</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;-T&quot; filetest recognizes UTF-8 encoded files as &quot;text&quot;</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>system()</code>, backticks and pipe open now reflect <code>exec()</code> failure</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Improved diagnostics</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Diagnostics follow STDERR</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>More consistent close-on-exec behavior</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt><code>syswrite()</code> ease-of-use</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Better syntax checks on parenthesized unary operators</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Bit operators support full native integer width</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Improved security features</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>More functional bareword prototype (*)</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>&quot;require&quot; and &quot;do&quot; may be overridden</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>$^X variables may now have names longer than one character</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>New variable $^C reflects &quot;-c&quot; switch</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>New variable $^V contains Perl version as a string</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Optional Y2K warnings</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Arrays now always interpolate into double-quoted strings</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>@- and @+ provide starting/ending offsets of regex matches</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>

<dl>
<dt>Perl Source Incompatibilities</dt>
<dd> <p> CHECK is a new keyword, Treatment of list slices of undef has changed, Format of <code>$English::PERL_VERSION</code> is different, Literals of the form <code>1.2.3</code> parse differently, Possibly changed pseudo-random number generator, Hashing function for hash keys has changed, <code>&quot;undef&quot;</code> fails on read only values, Close-on-exec bit may be set on pipe and socket handles, Writing <code>&quot;$$1&quot;</code> to mean <code>&quot;${$}1&quot;</code> is unsupported, <code>delete()</code>, <code>each()</code>, <code>values()</code> and <code>&quot;\(%h)&quot;</code>, vec(EXPR,OFFSET,BITS) enforces powers-of-two BITS, Text of some diagnostic output has changed, <code>&quot;%@&quot;</code> has been removed, Parenthesized <code>not()</code> behaves like a list operator, Semantics of bareword prototype <code>&quot;(*)&quot;</code> have changed, Semantics of bit operators may have changed on 64-bit platforms, More builtins taint their results </p>
 </dd>
<dt>C Source Incompatibilities</dt>
<dd> <p> <code>&quot;PERL_POLLUTE&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;PERL_IMPLICIT_CONTEXT&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;PERL_POLLUTE_MALLOC&quot;</code> </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Compatible C Source API Changes</dt>
<dd> <p> <code>&quot;PATCHLEVEL&quot;</code> is now <code>&quot;PERL_VERSION&quot;</code> </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Binary Incompatibilities</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Known Problems</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Arrow operator and arrays</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Experimental features</dt>
<dd> <p> Threads, Unicode, 64-bit support, Lvalue subroutines, Weak references, The pseudo-hash data type, The Compiler suite, Internal implementation of file globbing, The DB module, The regular expression code constructs: </p>
 </dd></dl>

 </dd>

<dd>  </dd>

<dd>  </dd></dl>

 </dd>

<h3>perlexperiment - A listing of experimental features in Perl</h3>
<dl>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Current experiments</dt>
<dd> <p> Smart match (<code>&quot;~~&quot;</code>), Pluggable keywords, Regular Expression Set Operations, Subroutine signatures, Aliasing via reference, The ``const&#8217;&#8216; attribute, use re &#8216;strict&#8217;;, The &lt;:win32&gt; IO pseudolayer, Declaring a reference to a variable, There is an <code>&quot;installhtml&quot;</code> target in the Makefile, Unicode in Perl on EBCDIC, Script runs, Alphabetic assertions </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Accepted features</dt>
<dd> <p> 64-bit support, die accepts a reference, DB module, Weak references, Internal file glob, <code>fork()</code> emulation, -Dusemultiplicity -Duseithreads, Support for long doubles, The <code>&quot;\N&quot;</code> regex character class, <code>&quot;(?{code})&quot;</code> and <code>&quot;(??{ code })&quot;</code>, Linux abstract Unix domain sockets, Lvalue subroutines, Backtracking control verbs, The &lt;:pop&gt; IO pseudolayer, <code>&quot;\s&quot;</code> in regexp matches vertical tab, Postfix dereference syntax, Lexical subroutines, String- and number-specific bitwise operators </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Removed features</dt>
<dd> <p> 5.005-style threading, perlcc, The pseudo-hash data type, GetOpt::Long Options can now take multiple values at once (experimental), Assertions, Test::Harness::Straps, <code>&quot;legacy&quot;</code>, Lexical <code>$_</code>, Array and hash container functions accept references, <code>&quot;our&quot;</code> can have an experimental optional attribute <code>&quot;unique&quot;</code> </p>
 </dd></dl>
</dl>

<dl>
<dt>Prerequisites for running Perl 5.22.1 under AmigaOS 4.1</dt>
<dd> <p> <code>AmigaOS 4.1 update 6 with all updates applied as of 9th October 2013</code>, <code>newlib.library version 53.28 or greater</code>, <code>AmigaOS </code><code>SDK</code>, <code>abc-shell</code> </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Starting Perl programs under AmigaOS 4.1</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Limitations of Perl under AmigaOS 4.1</dt>
<dd> <p> <code>Nested Piped programs can crash when run from older abc-shells</code>, <code>Incorrect or unexpected command line unescaping</code>, <code>Starting subprocesses via open has limitations</code>, If you find any other limitations or bugs then let me know </p>
 </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>INSTALLATION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Amiga Specific Modules</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Amiga::ARexx</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Amiga::Exec</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>BUILDING</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>CHANGES</dt>
<dd> <p> <code>August 2015</code>, Port to Perl 5.22, Add handling of NIL: to <code>afstat()</code>, Fix inheritance of environment variables by subprocesses, Fix exec, and exit in ``forked&#8217;&#8216; subprocesses, Fix issue with newlib&#8217;s unlink, which could cause infinite loops, Add <code>flock()</code> emulation using IDOS-&gt;LockRecord thanks to Tony Cook for the suggestion, Fix issue where kill was using the wrong kind of process ID, <code>27th November 2013</code>, Create new installation system based on installperl links and Amiga protection bits now set correctly, Pod now defaults to text, File::Spec should now recognise an Amiga style absolute path as well as an Unix style one. Relative paths must always be Unix style, <code>20th November 2013</code>, Configured to use SDK:Local/C/perl to start standard scripts, Added Amiga::Exec module with support for <code>Wait()</code> and AmigaOS signal numbers, <code>10th October 13</code> </p>
 </dd>
<dt>SEE ALSO</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>

<h3>experimental - Experimental features made easy</h3>
<dl>
<dt>VERSION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>SYNOPSIS</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd> <p> <code>&quot;array_base&quot;</code> - allow the use of <code>$[</code> to change the starting index of <code>@array</code>, <code>&quot;autoderef&quot;</code> - allow push, each, keys, and other built-ins on references, <code>&quot;bitwise&quot;</code> - allow the new stringwise bit operators, <code>&quot;const_attr&quot;</code> - allow the :const attribute on subs, <code>&quot;lexical_topic&quot;</code> - allow the use of lexical <code>$_</code> via <code>&quot;my $_&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;lexical_subs&quot;</code> - allow the use of lexical subroutines, <code>&quot;postderef&quot;</code> - allow the use of postfix dereferencing expressions, including in interpolating strings, <code>&quot;re_strict&quot;</code> - enables strict mode in regular expressions, <code>&quot;refaliasing&quot;</code> - allow aliasing via <code>&quot;\$x = \$y&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;regex_sets&quot;</code> - allow extended bracketed character classes in regexps, <code>&quot;signatures&quot;</code> - allow subroutine signatures (for named arguments), <code>&quot;smartmatch&quot;</code> - allow the use of <code>&quot;~~&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;switch&quot;</code> - allow the use of <code>&quot;~~&quot;</code>, given, and when, <code>&quot;win32_perlio&quot;</code> - allows the use of the :win32 IO layer </p>
 </dd>

<h3>ExtUtils::MakeMaker::FAQ - Frequently Asked Questions About MakeMaker</h3>
<dl>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Module Installation</dt>
<dd> <p> How do I install a module into my home directory?, How do I get MakeMaker and Module::Build to install to the same place?, How do I keep from installing man pages?, How do I use a module without installing it?, How can I organize tests into subdirectories and have them run?, PREFIX vs INSTALL_BASE from Module::Build::Cookbook, Generating *.pm files with substitutions eg of <code>$VERSION</code> </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Common errors and problems</dt>
<dd> <p> ``No rule to make target `/usr/lib/perl5/CORE/config.h&#8217;, needed by `Makefile&#8217;&#8216;&#8216; </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Philosophy and History</dt>
<dd> <p> Why not just use &lt;insert other build config tool here&gt;?, What is Module::Build and how does it relate to MakeMaker?, pure perl.<code>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;</code>no make, no<br/>
 shell commands, easier to customize, cleaner internals, less cruft </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Module Writing</dt>
<dd> <p> How do I keep my <code>$VERSION</code> up to date without resetting it manually?, What&#8217;s this <i></i><i>META</i><i>.yml</i> thing and how did it get in my <i></i><i>MANIFEST</i><i></i>?!, How do I delete everything not in my <i></i><i>MANIFEST</i><i></i>?, Which tar should I use on Windows?, Which zip should I use on Windows for &#8216;[ndg]make zipdist&#8217;? </p>
 </dd>
<dt>XS</dt>
<dd> <p> How do I prevent ``object version X.XX does not match bootstrap parameter Y.YY&#8217;&#8216; errors?, How do I make two or more XS files coexist in the same directory?, XSMULTI, Separate directories, Bootstrapping </p>
 </dd></dl>
</dl>

<dt>FUNCTIONS</dt>

<dt>$path = can_run( PROGRAM );</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>$ok | ($ok, $err, $full_buf, $stdout_buff, $stderr_buff) = run( command =&gt; COMMAND, [verbose =&gt; BOOL, buffer =&gt; \$SCALAR, timeout =&gt; DIGIT] );</dt>
<dd> <p> command, verbose, buffer, timeout, success, error message, full_buffer, out_buffer, error_buffer </p>
 </dd>
<dt>$hashref = run_forked( COMMAND, { child_stdin =&gt; SCALAR, timeout =&gt; DIGIT, stdout_handler =&gt; CODEREF, stderr_handler =&gt; CODEREF} );</dt>
<dd> <p> <code>&quot;timeout&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;child_stdin&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;stdout_handler&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;stderr_handler&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;wait_loop_callback&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;discard_output&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;terminate_on_parent_sudden_death&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;exit_code&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;timeout&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;stdout&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;stderr&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;merged&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;err_msg&quot;</code> </p>
 </dd>
<dt>$q = QUOTE</dt>
<dd>  </dd>

<h3>Pod::Checker - check pod documents for syntax errors</h3>
<dl>
<dt>SYNOPSIS</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>OPTIONS/ARGUMENTS</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt><code>podchecker()</code></dt>
<dd> <p> <code>-warnings</code> =&gt; <i>val</i>, <code>-quiet</code> =&gt; <i>val</i> </p>
 </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>DIAGNOSTICS</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Errors</dt>
<dd> <p> empty =headn, =over on line <i>N</i> without closing =back, You forgot a &#8216;=back&#8217; before &#8216;=head <i>N</i>&#8216;, =over is the last thing in the document?!, &#8216;=item&#8217; outside of any &#8216;=over&#8217;, =back without =over, Can&#8217;t have a 0 in =over <i>N</i>, =over should be: &#8216;=over&#8217; or &#8216;=over positive_number&#8217;, =begin <i></i><i>TARGET</i><i></i> without matching =end <i></i><i>TARGET</i><i></i>, =begin without a target?, =end <i></i><i>TARGET</i><i></i> without matching =begin, &#8216;=end&#8217; without a target?, &#8216;=end <i></i><i>TARGET</i><i></i>&#8216; is invalid, =end <i></i><i>CONTENT</i><i></i> doesn&#8217;t match =begin <i></i><i>TARGET</i><i></i>, =for without a target?, unresolved internal link <i></i><i>NAME</i><i></i>, Unknown directive: <i></i><i>CMD</i><i></i>, Deleting unknown formatting code <i></i><i>SEQ</i><i></i>, Unterminated <i></i><i>SEQ</i><i></i>&lt;&gt; sequence, An E&lt;...&gt; surrounding strange content, An empty E&lt;&gt;, An empty <code>&quot;L&lt;&gt;&quot;</code>, An empty X&lt;&gt;, A non-empty Z&lt;&gt;, Spurious text after =pod / =cut, =back doesn&#8217;t take any parameters, but you said =back <i></i><i>ARGUMENT</i><i></i>, =pod directives shouldn&#8217;t be over one line long!<code>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;</code>Ignoring all <i>N</i> lines of content, =cut found<br/>
 outside a pod block, Invalid =encoding syntax: <i></i><i>CONTENT</i><i></i> </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Warnings</dt>
<dd> <p> nested commands <i></i><i>CMD</i><i></i>&lt;...<i></i><i>CMD</i><i></i>&lt;...&gt;...&gt;, multiple occurrences (<i>N</i>) of link target <i>name</i>, line containing nothing but whitespace in paragraph, =item has no contents, You can&#8217;t have =items (as at line <i>N</i>) unless the first thing after the =over is an =item, Expected &#8216;=item <i></i><i>EXPECTED VALUE</i><i></i>&#8216;, Expected &#8216;=item *&#8216;, Possible =item type mismatch: &#8216;<i>x</i>&#8216; found leading a supposed definition =item, You have &#8216;=item x&#8217; instead of the expected &#8216;=item <i>N</i>&#8216;, Unknown E content in E&lt;<i></i><i>CONTENT</i><i></i>&gt;, empty =over/=back block, empty section in previous paragraph, Verbatim paragraph in NAME section, =head <i>n</i> without preceding higher level </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Hyperlinks</dt>
<dd> <p> ignoring leading/trailing whitespace in link, alternative text/node &#8216;%s&#8217; contains non-escaped | or / </p>
 </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>RETURN VALUE</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>EXAMPLES</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>SCRIPTS</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>INTERFACE</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<p> <code>&quot;Pod::Checker-&gt;new( %options )&quot;</code> </p>
<p> <code>&quot;$checker-&gt;poderror( @args )&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;$checker-&gt;poderror( {%opts}, @args )&quot;</code> </p>
<p> <code>&quot;$checker-&gt;num_errors()&quot;</code> </p>
<p> <code>&quot;$checker-&gt;num_warnings()&quot;</code> </p>
<p> <code>&quot;$checker-&gt;name()&quot;</code> </p>
<p> <code>&quot;$checker-&gt;node()&quot;</code> </p>
<p> <code>&quot;$checker-&gt;idx()&quot;</code> </p>
<p> <code>&quot;$checker-&gt;hyperlinks()&quot;</code> </p>
<p> <code>line()</code> </p>
<p> <code>type()</code> </p>
<p> <code>page()</code> </p>
<p> <code>node()</code> </p>
<dl>
<dt>AUTHOR</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>

<h3>Pod::Perldoc::GetOptsOO - Customized option parser for Pod::Perldoc</h3>
<dl>
<dt>SYNOPSIS</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd> <p> Call Pod::Perldoc::GetOptsOO::getopts($object, \@ARGV, <code>$truth</code>), Given -n, if there&#8217;s a opt_n_with, it&#8217;ll call <code>$object</code>-&gt;opt_n_with( ARGUMENT ) (e.g., ``-n foo&#8217;&#8216; =&gt; <code>$object</code>-&gt;opt_n_with(&#8216;foo&#8217;).<code>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;<wbr>&nbsp;</code>Ditto ``-nfoo&#8217;&#8216;), Otherwise<br/>
 (given -n) if there&#8217;s an opt_n, we&#8217;ll call it <code>$object</code>-&gt;opt_n($truth) (Truth defaults to 1), Otherwise we try calling <code>$object</code>-&gt;handle_unknown_option(&#8216;n&#8217;) (and we increment the error count by the return value of it), If there&#8217;s no handle_unknown_option, then we just warn, and then increment the error counter </p>
 </dd>
<dt>SEE ALSO</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>COPYRIGHT AND DISCLAIMERS</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>AUTHOR</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>

<h3>TAP::Parser::Scheduler - Schedule tests during parallel testing</h3>
<dl>
<dt>VERSION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>SYNOPSIS</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>METHODS</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>Class Methods</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>Rules data structure</dt>
<dd> <p> By default, all tests are eligible to be run in parallel. Specifying any of your own rules removes this one, ``First match wins&#8217;&#8216;. The first rule that matches a test will be the one that applies, Any test which does not match a rule will be run in sequence at the end of the run, The existence of a rule does not imply selecting a test. You must still specify the tests to run, Specifying a rule to allow tests to run in parallel does not make the run in parallel. You still need specify the number of parallel <code>&quot;jobs&quot;</code> in your Harness object </p>
 </dd></dl>
</dl>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd></dl>
<dt>Instance Methods</dt>
<dd>  </dd></dl>

<h3>Test2 - Framework for writing test tools that all work together.</h3>
<dl>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>WHAT IS NEW?</dt>
<dd> <p> Easier to test new testing tools, Better diagnostics capabilities, Event driven, More complete API, Support for output other than TAP, Subtest implementation is more sane, Support for threading/forking </p>
 </dd></dl>
</dl>

<dd>  </dd></dl>

 </dd>

<h3>Test2::API::Context - Object to represent a testing context.</h3>
<dl>
<dt>DESCRIPTION</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>SYNOPSIS</dt>
<dd>  </dd>
<dt>CRITICAL DETAILS</dt>
<dd> <p> you MUST always use the <code>context()</code> sub from Test2::API, You MUST always release the context when done with it, You MUST NOT pass context objects around, You MUST NOT store or cache a context for later, You SHOULD obtain your context as soon as possible in a given tool </p>
 </dd>
<dt>METHODS</dt>
<dd> <p> <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;done_testing;, <code>$clone</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;<code>snapshot()</code>, <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;<code>release()</code>, <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;throw($message), <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;alert($message), <code>$stack</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;<code>stack()</code>, <code>$hub</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;<code>hub()</code>, <code>$dbg</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;<code>trace()</code>, <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;do_in_context(\&amp;code, <code>@args</code>);, <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;<code>restore_error_vars()</code>, $! = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;<code>errno()</code>, $? = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;<code>child_error()</code>, $@ = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;<code>eval_error()</code> </p>
 </dd>
<dl>
<dd>  </dd>
<dl>
<dt>EVENT PRODUCTION METHODS</dt>
<dd> <p> <code>$event</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;<code>pass()</code>, <code>$event</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;pass($name), <code>$true</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;<code>pass_and_release()</code>, <code>$true</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;pass_and_release($name), my <code>$event</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;<code>fail()</code>, my <code>$event</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;fail($name), my <code>$event</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;fail($name, <code>@diagnostics</code>), my <code>$false</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;<code>fail_and_release()</code>, my <code>$false</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;fail_and_release($name), my <code>$false</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;fail_and_release($name, <code>@diagnostics</code>), <code>$event</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;ok($bool, <code>$name</code>), <code>$event</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;ok($bool, <code>$name</code>, \@on_fail), <code>$event</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;note($message), <code>$event</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;diag($message), <code>$event</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;plan($max), <code>$event</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;plan(0, &#8216;SKIP&#8216;, <code>$reason</code>), <code>$event</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;skip($name, <code>$reason</code>);, <code>$event</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;bail($reason), <code>$event</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;send_ev2(%facets), <code>$event</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;build_e2(%facets), <code>$event</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;send_ev2_and_release($Type, <code>%parameters</code>), <code>$event</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;send_event($Type, <code>%parameters</code>), <code>$event</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;build_event($Type, <code>%parameters</code>), <code>$event</code> = <code>$ctx</code>-&gt;send_event_and_release($Type, <code>%parameters</code>) </p>
 </dd></dl>

<dt>DIAGNOSTICS</dt>
<dd> <pre><code> C&lt;Did not find a suitable bracket: &quot;%s&quot;&gt;, C&lt;Did not find prefix: /%s/&gt;,
C&lt;Did not find opening bracket after prefix: &quot;%s&quot;&gt;, C&lt;No quotelike
operator found after prefix: &quot;%s&quot;&gt;, C&lt;Unmatched closing bracket: &quot;%c&quot;&gt;,
C&lt;Unmatched opening bracket(s): &quot;%s&quot;&gt;, C&lt;Unmatched embedded quote (%s)&gt;,
C&lt;Did not find closing delimiter to match '%s'&gt;, C&lt;Mismatched closing
bracket: expected &quot;%c&quot; but found &quot;%s&quot;&gt;, C&lt;No block delimiter found after
quotelike &quot;%s&quot;&gt;, C&lt;Did not find leading dereferencer&gt;, C&lt;Bad identifier
after dereferencer&gt;, C&lt;Did not find expected opening bracket at %s&gt;,
C&lt;Improperly nested codeblock at %s&gt;, C&lt;Missing second block for quotelike
&quot;%s&quot;&gt;, C&lt;No match found for opening bracket&gt;, C&lt;Did not find opening tag:
/%s/&gt;, C&lt;Unable to construct closing tag to match: /%s/&gt;, C&lt;Found invalid
nested tag: %s&gt;, C&lt;Found unbalanced nested tag: %s&gt;, C&lt;Did not find closing
tag&gt;
</code></pre>
 </dd>

<dt>Methods for Collation</dt>
<dd> <p> <code>&quot;@sorted = $Collator-&gt;sort(@not_sorted)&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;$result = $Collator-&gt;cmp($a, $b)&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;$result = $Collator-&gt;eq($a, $b)&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;$result = $Collator-&gt;ne($a, $b)&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;$result = $Collator-&gt;lt($a, $b)&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;$result = $Collator-&gt;le($a, $b)&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;$result = $Collator-&gt;gt($a, $b)&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;$result = $Collator-&gt;ge($a, $b)&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;$sortKey = $Collator-&gt;getSortKey($string)&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;$sortKeyForm = $Collator-&gt;viewSortKey($string)&quot;</code> </p>
 </dd>

<dd> <p> <code>&quot;$position = $Collator-&gt;index($string, $substring[, $position])&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;($position, $length) = $Collator-&gt;index($string, $substring[, $position])&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;$match_ref = $Collator-&gt;match($string, $substring)&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;($match) = $Collator-&gt;match($string, $substring)&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;@match = $Collator-&gt;gmatch($string, $substring)&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;$count = $Collator-&gt;subst($string, $substring, $replacement)&quot;</code>, <code>&quot;$count = $Collator-&gt;gsubst($string, $substring, $replacement)&quot;</code> </p>
 </dd>
<dt>Other Methods</dt>
